{"path":"vok-rest/src/main/kotlin/eu/vaadinonkotlin/rest/JavalinSupport.kt","prefix":"package eu.vaadinonkotlin.rest\n\nimport com.github.vokorm.KEntity\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.Dao\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.Property\nimport com.google.gson.Gson\nimport io.javalin.Javalin\nimport io.javalin.apibuilder.ApiBuilder\nimport io.javalin.apibuilder.CrudHandler\nimport io.javalin.config.JavalinConfig\nimport io.javalin.json.JavalinGson\nimport io.javalin.json.JsonMapper\nimport io.javalin.security.RouteRole\n\n/**\n * Configures [gson] as Javalin's [JsonMapper].\n */\npublic fun JavalinConfig.gsonMapper(gson: Gson) {\n    jsonMapper(JavalinGson(gson))\n}\n\n/**\n * Configures a CRUD handler to a particular endpoint, e.g. `javalin.crud(\"/rest/users\", User.getCrudHandler(false))`. The following endpoints are published:\n * * `GET /rest/users` returns all users\n * * `GET /rest/users/22` returns one user\n * * `POST /rest/users` will create an user\n * * `PATCH /rest/users/22` will update an user\n * * `DELETE /rest/users/22` will delete an user\n *\n * For details on query parameters for sorting, filtering, paging and count retrieval please see the [VokOrmCrudHandler] class itself.\n *\n * @param path the base path where the endpoints are exposed\n * @param crudHandler retrieves bean instances; you can use [getCrudHandler] to automatically provide instances of vok-orm entities.\n * @param permittedRoles only these roles are allowed to access abovementioned endpoints. See Javalin [io.javalin.security.AccessManager] and the Javalin documentation for more details.\n */\npublic fun Javalin.crud2(path: String, crudHandler: CrudHandler, permittedRoles: Set<RouteRole> = setOf()): Javalin = routes {\n    val p = path.trim('/')\n    if (p.contains('/')) {\n        ApiBuilder.path(p.substringBeforeLast('/')) {\n            ApiBuilder.crud(p.substringAfterLast('/') + \"/{id}\", crudHandler, *permittedRoles.toTypedArray())\n        }\n    } else {\n        ApiBuilder.crud(path, crudHandler, *permittedRoles.toTypedArray())\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates a CRUD Handler for this entity, providing instances of this entity over REST. You should simply pass the CRUD Handler into\n * the [crud2] function. A shorthand for creating the [VokOrmCrudHandler] for this entity.\n *\n * The [VokOrmCrudHandler.getAll] honors the following query parameters:\n * * `limit` and `offset` for result paging. Both must be 0 or greater; `limit` must be less than [maxLimit]\n * * `sort_by=-last_modified,+email,first_name` - a list of sorting clauses. Only those which appear in [allowSortColumns] are allowed.\n * Prepending a column name with `-` will sort DESC.\n * * To define filters, simply pass in column names with the values, for example `age=81`. You can also specify operators: one of\n * `eq:`, `lt:`, `lte:`, `gt:`, `gte:`, `ilike:`, `like:`, `isnull:`, `isnotnull:`, for example `age=lt:25`. You can pass single column name\n * multiple times to AND additional clauses, for example `name=ilike:martin&age=lte:70&age=gte:20&birthdate=isnull:&grade=5`. * @property method the request method, e.g. `\"GET\"`\n * @property requestUrl the URL requested from the server\n * @property response the response body received from the server, may provide further information to the nature of the failure.\n * May be blank.\n */\npublic class HttpResponseException(\n    public val statusCode: Int,\n    public val method: String,\n    public val requestUrl: String,\n    public val response: String,\n    cause: Throwable? = null\n) : IOException(\"$statusCode: $response\", cause) {\n    override fun toString(): String = \"${javaClass.simpleName}: $message HttpResponseException if the response is not in 200..299 ([Response.isSuccessful] returns false)\n * @throws IOException on I/O error.\n */\npublic fun <T> HttpResponse<T>.checkOk(): HttpResponse<T> {\n    if (!isSuccessful) {\n        val response = bodyAsString()\n        if (statusCode() == 404) throw FileNotFoundException(\"${statusCode()}: $response (${request().method()} ${request().uri()})\")\n        throw HttpResponseException(statusCode(), request().method(), request().uri().toString(), response)\n    }\n    return this\n}\n\n/**\n * True if [checkOk] is used, to check for\n * possible failure reported as HTTP status code, prior calling the block.\n * @param responseBlock runs on success. Both must be 0 or greater; `limit` must be less than [maxLimit]\n * * `sort_by=-last_modified,+email,first_name` - a list of sorting clauses. Only those which appear in [allowSortColumns] are allowed.\n *    Prepending a column name with `-` will sort DESC.\n * * To define filters, simply pass in column names with the values, for example `age=81`. You can also specify operators: one of\n *   `eq:`, `lt:`, `lte:`, `gt:`, `gte:`, `ilike:`, `like:`, `isnull:`, `isnotnull:`, for example `age=lt:25`. You can pass single column name\n *    multiple times to AND additional clauses, for example `name=ilike:martin&age=lte:70&age=gte:20&birthdate=isnull:&grade=5`. OR filters are not supported.\n * * `select=count` - if this is passed in, then instead of a list of matching objects a single number will be returned: the number of\n * records matching given filters.\n *\n * @property dao fetches items from the database.\n * @property maxLimit the maximum number of items permitted to be returned by [getAll]. If the client attempts to request more\n * items, then 400 BAD REQUEST is returned. Defaults to [Int.MAX_VALUE], definitely change this in production!\n * @property defaultLimit if `limit` is unspecified in [getAll] request, then return at most this number of items. References the [kotlin.reflect.KProperty1.name] of the entity.\n * @param allowFilterColumns if not null, only these columns are allowed to be filtered upon. Defaults to null. References the [kotlin.reflect.KProperty1.name] of the entity.\n */\npublic open class DaoOfAnyCrudHandler<T : Any>(\n    public val dao: DaoOfAny<T>,\n    public val maxLimit: Long = // grab fetchRange from the query\n        val limit = ctx.queryParamAsClass<Long>(\"limit\").getOrDefault(defaultLimit)\n        if (limit !in 0..maxLimit) throw BadRequestResponse(\"invalid limit $limit, must be 0..$maxLimit\")\n        val offset = ctx.queryParamAsClass<Long>(\"offset\").getOrDefault(0)\n        if (offset < 0) throw BadRequestResponse(\"invalid offset $offset, must be 0 or greater\")\n\n\n        // grab sorting from the query\n        val sortByParam = ctx.queryParam(\"sort_by\") ?: \"\"\n        val sortedColumns = sortByParam.split(\",\").filter { it.isNotBlank() }\n BadRequestResponse(\"Cannot sort by ${sortedColumn}: allowed sorting columns are $allowSortColumns\")\n            }\n        }\n        val sortBy: List<OrderBy> = sortedColumns.map {   }\n            ctx.queryParams(filterColumn).forEach { value ->\n                val c = restFilterToCondition(value, getProperty(filterColumn) as Property<Any>)\n                filter = filter.and(c)\n            }\n        }\n\n        // fetch the data\n        if (ctx.queryParam(\"select\") == \"count\") {\n            val count = dao.countBy(filter)\n            ctx.result(count.toString())\n        } else {\n            val result = dao.findAllBy(filter, sortBy, offset, limit)\n            ctx.json(result)\n        }\n    }\n\n    private fun -> getProperty(sortQuery.substring(1)).asc()\n        sortQuery.startsWith(\"-\") -> getProperty(sortQuery.substring(1)).desc()\n        else -> getProperty(sortQuery).asc()\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * prop.valueType))\n            value.startsWith(\"lt:\") -> prop.lt(convertToDatabase(value.substring(3), prop.valueType))\n            value.startsWith(\"ne:\") -> prop.ne(convertToDatabase(value.substring(3), prop.valueType))\n            value.startsWith(\"isnull:\") -> prop.isNull\n            value.startsWith(\"isnotnull:\") -> prop.isNotNull\n            value.startsWith(\"like:\") -> prop.like(value.substring(5))\n            value.startsWith(\"ilike:\") -> prop.likeIgnoreCase(value.substring(6))\n            value.startsWith(\"fulltext:\") -> Number::class.java.isAssignableFrom(expectedClass.nonPrimitive) -> BigDecimal(nvalue)\n            Date::class.java.isAssignableFrom(expectedClass) -> Instant.ofEpochMilli(nvalue.toLong())\n            expectedClass == LocalDate::class.java || expectedClass == LocalDateTime::class.java || expectedClass == Instant::class.java -> Instant.ofEpochMilli(nvalue.toLong())\n            else -> nvalue\n Registering same listener multiple times has no further effect.\n     *\n     * The equality of the listener is measured by using the standard [Any.equals] and [Any.hashCode].\n     */\n    public fun add(listener: T) {\n        listeners.add(listener)\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes the listener. Removing same listener multiple times has no further effect. Does nothing\n     * if the listener has not yet been registered.\n     *\n     * The equality of the listener is measured by using the standard [Any.equals] and [Any.hashCode].\n     */\n    public fun remove(listener: T) {\n        listeners.remove(listener)\n    }\n\n    override fun toString(): String =\n            \"Listeners($listenerType, listeners=$listeners)\"\n\n    /**\n     * Use the returned value to fire particular event to all listeners.\n     *\n     * Returns a proxy of type [T]. Any method call on this proxy is propagated to all\n     * listeners.\n     *\n     * Not serializable!\n     */\n    @Suppress(\"","middle":"","suffix":"UNCHECKED_CAST\")\n    public val fire: T get() = Proxy.newProxyInstance(\n        listenerType.classLoader,\n        arrayOf(listenerType)\n    ) { _, method: Method, args: Array<Any>? ->\n        if (method.name == \"toString\") {\n            \"fire($this}\"\n        } else {\n            for (listener in listeners) {\n                if (args == null) {\n                    method.invoke(listener)\n                } else {\n                    method.invoke(listener, *args)\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    } as T\n}","contexts":[{"path":"vok-framework/src/main/kotlin/eu/vaadinonkotlin/AsyncExecutor.kt","content":"public fun <R> async(logException: Boolean = true, block: () -> R): Future<R> = {"path":"vok-rest-client/src/main/kotlin/eu/vaadinonkotlin/restclient/CrudClient.kt","prefix":"package eu.vaadinonkotlin.restclient\n\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.OrderBy\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.Property\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.And\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.Condition\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.Eq\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.Expression\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.FullTextCondition\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.IsNotNull\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.IsNull\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.Like\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.LikeIgnoreCase\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.jdbiorm.condition.Op\nimport com.gitlab.mvysny.uribuilder.net.URIBuilder\nimport com.vaadin.flow.data.provider.AbstractBackEndDataProvider\nimport com.vaadin.flow.data.provider.Query\nimport com.vaadin.flow.data.provider.QuerySortOrder\nimport com.vaadin.flow.data.provider.SortDirection\nimport eu.vaadinonkotlin.MediaType\nimport java.net.URI\nimport java.net.http.HttpClient\nimport java.net.http.HttpRequest\nimport java.util.stream.Stream\n\n/**\n * Uses the CRUD endpoint and serves instances of given item of type [itemClass] over given [client] using [VokRestClient.gson].\n * Expect the CRUD endpoint to be exposed in the following manner:\n * * `GET /rest/users` returns all users\n * * `GET /rest/users?select=count` returns a single number - the count of all users. This is only necessary for [getCount]\n * or if you plan to use this client as a backend for Vaadin Grid.\n * * `GET /rest/users/22` returns one users\n * * `POST /rest/users` will create an user\n * * `PATCH /rest/users/22` will update an user\n * * `DELETE /rest/users/22` will delete an user\n *\n * Paging/sorting/filtering is supported: the following query parameters will simply be added to the \"get all\" URL request:\n *\n * * `limit` and `offset` for result paging. Both must be 0 or greater. The server may impose max value limit on the `limit` parameter.\n * * `sort_by=-last_modified,+email,first_name` - a list of sorting clauses. The server may restrict sorting by only a selected subset of properties.\n * * The filters are simply converted to query parameters, for example `age=81`. [Op]s are also supported: the value will be prefixed with a special operator prefix:\n * `eq:`, `lt:`, `lte:`, `gt:`, `gte:`, `ilike:`, `like:`, `isnull:`, `isnotnull:`, for example `age=lt:25`. A full example is `name=ilike:martin&age=lte:70&age=gte:20&birthdate=isnull:&grade=5`.\n * OR filters are not supported - passing [Or] will cause [getAll] to throw [IllegalArgumentException].\n *\n * Since this client is also a [DataProvider], you can use this class to feed data into Vaadin Grid or ComboBox etc:\n * ```\n * val crud = CrudClient(\"http://localhost:8080/rest/person/\", Person::class.java)\n * grid.dataProvider = crud\n * ```\n * @property baseUrl the base URL, such as `http://localhost:8080/rest/users/`, must end with a slash.\n * @property converter used to convert filter values to strings passable as query parameters. Defaults to [QueryParameterConverter] with system-default\n * zone; it is pretty much recommended to set a specific time zone.\n * @property client which HTTP client to use, defaults to [VokRestClient.httpClient].\n * @property converter converts filter values to strings when querying for data.\n * Defaults to [QueryParameterConverter].\n */\npublic open class CrudClient<T: Any>(\n    public val baseUrl: String,\n    public val itemClass: Class<T>,\n    public val client: HttpClient = VokRestClient.httpClient,\n    public val converter: Converter<in Any, String> = QueryParameterConverter()) : AbstractBackEndDataProvider<T, Condition>() {\n    init {\n        require(baseUrl.endsWith(\"/\")) { \"$baseUrl must end with /\" }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Fetches data from the back end. The items must match given [filter]. This function does exactly the same as [fetch].\n     * @param filter optional filter which defines filtering to be used for counting the\n     * number of items. If null all items are considered.\n     * @param sortBy optionally sort the beans according to given fields. By default sorts ASC; if you prepend the field with the \"-\"\n     * character the sorting will be DESC.\n     * @param range offset and limit to fetch\n     * @return a list of items matching the query, may be empty.\n     */\n    private fun getAll(filter: Condition = Condition.NO_CONDITION, sortBy: List<OrderBy> = listOf(), offset: Long, limit: Long): List<T> {\n        val url: URI = baseUrl.buildUrl {\n            addParameter(\"offset\", offset.toString())\n            addParameter(\"limit\", limit.toString())\n            if (sortBy.isNotEmpty()) {\n                addParameter(\"sort_by\", sortBy.joinToString(\",\") { \"${if(it.order == OrderBy.Order.ASC)\"+\" else \"-\"}${it.property.name}\" })\n            }\n            addFilterQueryParameters(filter)\n        }\n        val request: HttpRequest = url.buildRequest()\n        return client.exec(request) { response -> response.jsonArray(itemClass) }\n    }\n\n    private fun URIBuilder.addFilterQueryParameters(condition: Condition) {\n        fun opToRest(op: Op.Operator): String = when (op) {\n            Op.Operator.EQ -> \"eq\"\n            Op.Operator.GE -> \"gte\"\n            Op.Operator.GT -> \"gt\"\n            Op.Operator.LE -> \"lte\"\n            Op.Operator.LT -> \"lt\"\n            Op.Operator.NE -> \"ne\"\n        }\n\n        fun Expression<*>.getPropertyName(): String {\n            val propName = (this as Property<*>).name.name\n            require(propName != \"limit\" && propName != \"offset\" && propName != \"sort_by\" && propName != \"select\") {\n                \"cannot filter on reserved query parameter name $propName\"\n            }\n            return propName\n        }\n        fun Expression<*>.getValue(): String? {\n            val value = (this as Expression.Value<*>).value ?: return null\n            return converter.convert(value)\n        }\n\n        when {\n            condition == Condition.NO_CONDITION -> return\n            condition is Eq -> addParameter(condition.arg1.getPropertyName(), condition.arg2.getValue())\n            condition is IsNotNull -> addParameter(condition.arg.getPropertyName(), \"isnotnull:\")\n            condition is IsNull -> addParameter(condition.arg.getPropertyName(), \"isnull:\")\n            condition is Like -> addParameter(condition.arg1.getPropertyName(), \"like:\" + condition.arg2.getValue())\n            condition is LikeIgnoreCase -> addParameter(condition.arg1.getPropertyName(), \"ilike:\" + condition.arg2.getValue())\n            condition is Op -> addParameter(condition.arg1.getPropertyName(), opToRest(condition.operator) + \":\" + condition.arg2.getValue())\n            condition is FullTextCondition -> addParameter(condition.arg.getPropertyName(), \"fulltext:\" + condition.query)\n            condition is And -> { addFilterQueryParameters(condition.condition1); addFilterQueryParameters(condition.condition2) }\n            else -> throw IllegalArgumentException(\"Unsupported condition $condition\")\n        }\n    }\n\n    public fun getOne(id: String): T {\n        val request: HttpRequest = \"$baseUrl$id\".buildUrl().buildRequest()\n        return client.exec(request) { response -> response.json(itemClass) }\n    }\n\n    public fun create(entity: T) {\n        val json: String = VokRestClient.gson.toJson(entity)\n        val request: HttpRequest = baseUrl.buildUrl()","middle":".buildRequest { post(json, MediaType.jsonUtf8) }\n","suffix":"        client.exec(request) {}\n    }\n\n    public fun update(id: String, entity: T) {\n        val json: String = VokRestClient.gson.toJson(entity)\n        val request: HttpRequest = \"$baseUrl$id\".buildUrl().buildRequest { patch(json, MediaType.jsonUtf8) }\n        client.exec(request) {}\n    }\n\n    public fun delete(id: String) {\n        val request: HttpRequest = {"path":"vok-rest-client/src/main/kotlin/eu/vaadinonkotlin/restclient/Converter.kt","prefix":"package eu.vaadinonkotlin.restclient\n\nimport java.lang.IllegalArgumentException\nimport {\n    public fun convert(value: F): T\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts values of different typ","middle":"es properly to String, so that they can be consumed by the REST endpoints.\n","suffix":" * The default implementation uses the following algorithm:\n * * Converts all [Number] to their decimal format with comma as the decimal separator, e.g. \"54\" or \"2.25\"\n * * Convert all Date-like objects such as [Date], [LocalDate] and [LocalDateTime] to the UTC Epoch long (number of milliseconds since January 1, 1970, 00:00:00 GMT in UTC).\n * * Fails for everything else.\n * @param zoneId used to convert [LocalDate] and [LocalDateTime] to UTC Epoch.\n */\npublic open class QueryParameterConverter(public val zoneId: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault()) : Converter<Any, String> {\n    protected fun convertNumber(number: Number): String = when(number) {\n        is Int, is Short, is Byte, is Long, is BigInteger -> number.toString()\n        is BigDecimal -> number.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()\n        is Float -> convertNumber(number.toDouble())\n        is Double -> convertNumber(number.toBigDecimal())\n        else -> throw IllegalArgumentException(\"$number of type ${number.javaClass} is not supported\")\n    }\n\n    override fun convert(value: Any): String = when(value) {\n        is String -> value\n        is Number -> convertNumber(value)\n        is Date -> value.time.toString()\n        is LocalDate -> convert(LocalDateTime.of(value, LocalTime.MIN))\n        is LocalDateTime -> convert(value.atZone(zoneId))\n        is ZonedDateTime -> convert(value.toInstant())\n        is Instant -> value.toEpochMilli().toString()\n        else -> throw IllegalArgumentException(\"$value of type ${value.javaClass} is not {"path":"vok-framework/src/main/kotlin/eu/vaadinonkotlin/AsyncExecutor.kt","prefix":"package eu.vaadinonkotlin\n\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime\nimport java.time.LocalTime\nimport java.time.ZoneOffset\nimport java.util.concurrent.Callable\nimport java.util.concurrent.Future\nimport java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException\nimport java.util.concurrent.ScheduledFuture\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit\nimport kotlin.time.Duration\nimport kotlin.time.Duration.Companion.days\nimport kotlin.time.Duration.Companion.milliseconds\n\n/**\n * Submits a value-returning task for execution and returns a\n * Future representing the pending results of the task. The\n * [Future.get] method will return the task's result upon\n * successful completion.\n *\n * If you would like to immediately block waiting\n * for a task, you can use constructions of the form\n * `result = exec.submit(aCallable).get()`\n *\n * @param block the task to submit\n * @param <R> the type of the task's result\n * @param logException if true (default) and the [block] throws an exception, the exception is logged via slf4j.\n * This prevents the exception from being lost silently if nobody calls [Future.get].\n * @return a Future representing pending completion of the task\n * @throws RejectedExecutionException if the task cannot be\n *         scheduled for execution\n */\npublic fun <R> async(logException: Boolean = true, block: () -> R): Future<R> = VaadinOnKotlin.asyncExecutor.submit(Callable {\n    try {\n        block.invoke()\n    } catch (t: Throwable) {\n        // log the except","middle":"ion - if nobody is waiting on the Future, the exception would have been lost.\n        if (logException) {\n            LoggerFactory.getLogger(block::class.java)\n                .error(\"Async failed: $t\", t)\n        }\n","suffix":"        throw t\n    }\n})\n\n/**\n * Executes [command] periodically; first execution happens after the given initial delay, and subsequently with the given\n * period; that is executions will commence after\n * `initialDelay` then `initialDelay+period`, then\n * `initialDelay + 2 * period`, and so on.\n *\n * If any execution of the task\n * encounters an exception, subsequent executions are suppressed.\n * Otherwise, the task will only terminate via cancellation or\n * termination of the executor.  If any execution of this task\n * takes longer than its period, then subsequent executions\n * may start late, but will not concurrently execute.\n *\n * @param command the task to execute\n * @param initialDelay the time to delay first execution, in millis. You can use expressions like `5.days + 2.seconds` to compute this value.\n *                      If 0 or smaller, the task will be executed immediately.\n * @param period the period between successive executions, in millis.\n * @param logException if true (default) and the [command] throws an exception, the exception is logged via slf4j.\n * @return a ScheduledFuture representing pending completion of\n *         the task, and whose `get()` method will throw an\n *         exception upon cancellation\n * @throws RejectedExecutionException if the task cannot be\n *         scheduled for execution\n * @throws IllegalArgumentException if period less than or equal to zero\n */\npublic fun scheduleAtFixedRate(initialDelay: Duration, period: Duration, logException: Boolean = true, command: ()->Unit): ScheduledFuture<*>\n        = VaadinOnKotlin.asyncExecutor.scheduleAtFixedRate({\n    try {\n        command.invoke()\n    } catch (t: Throwable) {\n        // if nobody is using Future to wait for the result of this op, the exception is lost. better log it here.\n        if (logException) {\n            LoggerFactory.getLogger(command::class.java)\n                .error(\"Async failed: $t\", t)\n        }\n        throw t\n    }\n}, initialDelay.inWholeMilliseconds, period.inWholeMilliseconds, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)\n\n/**\n * Executes [command] periodically at given [time].\n *\n * If any execution of the task\n * encounters an exception, subsequent executions are suppressed.\n * Otherwise, the task will only terminate via cancellation or\n * termination of the executor. logException if true (default) and the [command] throws an exception, the exception is logged via slf4j.\n * @return a ScheduledFuture representing pending completion of\n *         the task, and whose `get()` method will throw an\n *         exception upon cancellation\n * @throws RejectedExecutionException if the task cannot be\n *         scheduled for execution\n * @throws IllegalArgumentException if period less than or equal to zero\n */\npublic fun scheduleAtFixedTime(time: LocalTime, logException: Boolean = true, command: ()->Unit): ScheduledFuture<*> {\n    val now = LocalDateTime.now()\n    var nextExecution = LocalDateTime.of(now.toLocalDate(), time)\n    if (nextExecution < now) {\n        nextExecution = nextExecution.plusDays(1L)\n    }\n    val initialDelay = nextExecution - now\n    return scheduleAtFixedRate(initialDelay, 1.days, logException, command)\n}\n\nprivate operator fun LocalDateTime.minus(other: LocalDateTime): Duration =\n
